#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_DANCER_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1C_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 61
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1E_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 81
			}
		}
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1F_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 101
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1G_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 121
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1H_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_8	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_8_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_1_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_2_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_3_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_4_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_5_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_6_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_7_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_8_upgrade	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_2
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_3
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_4
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_5
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_6
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_7
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_indianharem_menu_8						
		}					
}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		OR = {
			culture_group = hindusthani
			culture_group = western_aryan
			culture_group = turko_semitic
			culture_group = dravidian
			culture_group = central_indic
			culture_group = eastern_aryan
		}		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_harem_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_piety_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant harem 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = rajput
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = rajput
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = rajput
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = rajput
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = rajput
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = rajput
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = rajput
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = rajput
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant harem 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = sindhi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = sindhi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = sindhi
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sindhi
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sindhi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sindhi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_1c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.13.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = sindhi
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = sindhi
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_1c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.13.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA

		}

			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_2c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = malvi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = malvi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = malvi
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_2c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_2c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malvi
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malvi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malvi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_2c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.23.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = malvi
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = malvi
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_2c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA

			duration = 3650

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA

			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			duration = 3650

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA

		}

#grant harem 4a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.41
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.41.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.41.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_4A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_4a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = avadhi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = avadhi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = avadhi
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_4a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_4a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = avadhi
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = avadhi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = avadhi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

			duration = 3650

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_4a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_4a_DESC
				}			
			}

			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = vindhyan
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_4c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}

#grant harem 5a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.51
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.51.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.51.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_5A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_5a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kannada
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kannada
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = kannada
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_5a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_5a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kannada
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kannada
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kannada
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_5a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_5a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.51.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = kannada
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = kannada
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_5a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.51.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant harem 6a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.61
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.61.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_6A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = malayalam
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = malayalam
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = malayalam
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malayalam
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malayalam
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = malayalam
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_6a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_6a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.61.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = malayalam
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = malayalam
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_indianharem_6a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_indiamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.61.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives

		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = jharkhandi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = jharkhandi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = jharkhandi
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_7b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_7b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = jharkhandi
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = jharkhandi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = jharkhandi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

				hidden = yes

#grant harem 8c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.83
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.83.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.83.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_8C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_8c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = bihari
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = bihari
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = bihari
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_8c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_8c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bihari
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bihari
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bihari
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_8c
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_8c_DESC
				}			
			}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.93
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.93.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.93.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_9C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_9c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kochi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kochi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = kochi
				}
		}
			
	}

#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = india_concubine_9c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = india_concubine_9c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kochi
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kochi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kochi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_INDIA

			duration = 3650

				}

		}

			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTwoACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}

				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoJapaneseCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTwoCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremTwoCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTwoCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoJapaneseCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremFiveACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremFiveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremFiveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.700
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.700.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.700.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_7_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		#immediate for palace estate				
		}
		}

		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.700.a				
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = india_concubine_7a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = india_concubine_7a
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.710
			}
		}

		}
		}

			}

				}
				}

					}
				}
			}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {

	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_8C_4_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter
			value = 261
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_8C_1_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter
			value = 281
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_8C_5_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	goto = consortprovince

		ai = no
	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = india_concubine_8c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.830.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoJapaneseCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_navigator
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}

		which = cosmoIHaremEightCCounter
		value = 0
			}

			value = 280
			}

		}	
			}

		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_colonial_governor
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremNineCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremNineCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}

	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_10C_11_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	goto = consortprovince

		ai = no
	
	}

	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = india_concubine_10c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.1030.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoJapaneseCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTenCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremTenCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_diplomat
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoIHaremTenCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTenCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

			}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoIHaremTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.999
	title = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.999.t
	desc = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.999.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_INDIA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no			
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_indiaharemestate_events.999.a
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_1a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_1b
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_1b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_1c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_2a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_2a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_2b
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_2b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_2c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = india_concubine_3a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = india_concubine_3b
				duration =  -1
